## November 16, 2026 — AUTHOR DIRECTION: Grapple-range weapon, shield, initiation and escape rules

**Latest author-approved grapple-range principles (subject to the numerical escape procedure below being finalized):**
- **Close control and reach:** In a genuinely established grapple at inside/grapple distance, ordinary **Long-reach melee weapons cannot attack the grappling opponent**; **Medium-reach melee attacks against that opponent are one Naked-d20 hit-difficulty category harder**; Short-reach melee attacks and lawful unarmed attacks remain eligible normally. This one-category Medium penalty applies through the existing six-tier category ladder, not as a flat d20 modifier. Do not assume mere adjacent positioning, proximity, or a failed attempted hold creates a grapple. Established opportunity/reach-entry requirements and counteractions remain.
- **Ranged weapon handling:** Ordinary attacks with **two-handed ranged weapons cannot be made against the grappling opponent while mutually engaged in close grapple**; a **one-handed ranged weapon remains eligible** when its physical presentation, targeting and safe firing are actually possible. This is a handling constraint, not an invented universal firearm bonus.
- **Shields:** Attacks made by a grappler against the opponent they genuinely hold **bypass that opponent's normal shield Guard interception/block**; physical END, actual worn-body armor coverage/penetration, and independent non-shield defenses still apply. This does not grant a blanket exception to other opponents attacking that shield user or erase a shield's physical existence.
- **Initiation:** Establishing a grapple **requires an eligible attack check** under the established Naked-d20 hit-category system and real physical opportunity to enter/control the opponent. It is not automatic upon adjacency and does not grant a free second attack. Exact Full/Quick action assignment, whether a further holding check is needed, hand occupancy and resistance special cases remain to be finalized.
- **Escape:** An opponent may **attempt to escape on their own turn using a d20 roll** favoring the relative **STR and DEX** of holder and escaper, ideally mapped to the existing six hit-difficulty categories. On a **failed escape attempt**, the escaper **cannot leave their current square** by ordinary movement and incurs **one incoming Pressure Point**. Proposed PP expiry, overlap with other PP sources, escape attempt action cost, forced displacement and exactly how the ratio is calculated remain open; do not canonize values merely from testing discussion.

**Unapproved procedural candidate, for subsequent playtests:** Grapple Control `G = effective STR + effective DEX` for both parties; map `G_holder/G_escaper` to current Certain/Easy/Average/Hard/Very Hard/Impossible ratio thresholds (<0.50 / <0.80 / <1.25 / <1.50 / <3.00 / otherwise). Possible one-PP failure duration through start of escaper's next turn, nonstacking for repeated failures, is a **proposal only**. Do not replace approved Combat Speed calculation for normal attack accuracy/evasion. No change to approved physical Power formula or wound thresholds.

## November 16, 2026 — Power Attack self-Pressure and single-target identity APPROVED (LATEST)

**Latest explicit author approval supersedes the earlier harder-to-hit Power Attack.** **Power Attack** remains a **2-action Full Attack** and one permitted offensive attack action, but now has **NO innate additional hit-difficulty category**. It instead gives its **USER exactly one self-applied meaningful Pressure Point** for the reckless, committed blow, **from initiating the maneuver until the start of that user's next turn**. This is one total self-PP source, not a separate point per enemy, repeated roll, damage effect, or target. It contributes to incoming opponents' existing net-Pressure calculations only where actual credible adversarial threats can exploit it; distinct qualified sources may combine under existing net 1–2 / 3+ PP thresholds. It never grants a Pressure Point against the struck target or an automatic counterattack. It does not replace the existing defensive conditions, PP cancellation mechanisms or combat consequences.

**Power and target limits:** Power Attack still multiplies the attack's **otherwise lawful final physical Attack Power by ×1.25 ONCE after the established attribute, weapon F, material, Quality and permitted effect calculation**, before actual END / covered-AR Impact and injury thresholds. It **targets one single legitimately reachable enemy only**: no Sweep, Wide Sweep, Line, squad/group multi-target delivery or duplicate target packets, even if the weapon normally has those attack coverages. The weapon retains its real reach, attack form, armor penetration, geometry, stat/handling and all other valid rules for that *one* target. The attack may use an otherwise legal single shield bash or weapon as its existing action permits; it grants no new shield interception, block or free offhand role. It cannot be Quick Attack, Double Strike, another offensive attack action in the same turn, or an extra strike.

**Approved headshot combination:** A Power Attack can aim at one genuinely accessible vulnerable **protected helmet/head gap**, using the **existing +2 harder difficulty categories for that actual gap (AR25)** and **×1.25 head-gap final-Power bonus**. No separate Power Attack accuracy penalty is added. The independent Power multipliers yield **×1.25 ×1.25 = ×1.5625** of otherwise lawful final physical Attack Power; the **one self-PP against the attacker** still applies. A genuinely exposed head retains its **+1 harder category when required, AR0, and NO additional headshot Power bonus**; fully protected helmets without an accessible gap provide no bonus. Headshot accessibility and actual shielding remain physical conditions, not automatic entitlements. The earlier **Power Attack +1 harder category** and associated **three-category Power Attack-plus-head-gap** arithmetic are **historical, superseded by this author approval**.

**Tactical identity and remaining watchpoint:** Full Attack retains its ordinary accuracy and any genuine original multi-target attack coverage. Power Attack concentrates ×1.25 Power on *one* target while risking retaliation via self-PP, rather than reducing accuracy. This creates a possible one-on-one or no-retaliation advantage; evaluate organically in friends-led tabletop play, with no required AI tests. The alternative +50% Power Attack remains unapproved. No other formulas, equipment qualities, armor Ratings, enchantments, offhand roles or injury thresholds change.

---

## November 16, 2026 — Headshot precision damage APPROVED; Power Attack self-Pressure alternative UNDER REVIEW

**Latest explicit author approval of the preceding headshot study recommendation:** A **genuinely accessible protected helmet/head structural gap** that is physically vulnerable may be deliberately targeted under the already-approved **+2 harder hit-difficulty categories** with the gap's **actual AR 25** in place of the helmet's covered main-surface AR. **When that legal vulnerable head-gap called shot actually hits the intended region, multiply the otherwise final physical Attack Power by ×1.25 once**, after all lawful weapon/Quality/material adjustments and before the ordinary END/AR Impact and injury-stage resolution. **Only one selected legal target** can receive a declared headshot; ordinary Sweep/Wide Sweep/Line multi-target coverage does not multiply targeted headshots. No extra offensive action is created. A qualifying headgap must genuinely exist, be reachable and physically addressable; helmets, anatomy, shields and target position may make it inaccessible or undefinable. Do not invent a universal weak point, waive a shield or automatically achieve a successful headshot.

**Exposed and fully covered heads:** Targeting a **genuinely exposed head** follows ordinary approved exposed-location rules (**+1 difficulty category when precision is required; actual AR0**) but grants **no extra headshot Power multiplier**. A **fully protected helmet head surface without a genuine accessible structural gap** grants **no +25% headshot Power**, no unearned gap AR25, and no other special vulnerability from calling the location. Extremely tiny exposed openings still follow their separately approved **+3-category, AR0** procedure and do **not** gain the special head-gap Power bonus merely for being exposed. A creature lacking a physically relevant vulnerable head structure does not acquire one from this maneuver.

**Stacking with currently approved Power Attack:** Headshot targeting is a specific single-target attack choice, **not** a second attack action. An otherwise legal single **2-action Power Attack** may target the same genuine helmet/head gap. Under the *current approved Power Attack rule*, apply its **+1 harder category** in addition to the actual head-gap's **+2 categories** (three total; clamp at Impossible), then apply the two independent final-Power multipliers **×1.25 ×1.25 = ×1.5625** only if the strike lawfully connects. Power Attack still uses its approved **accuracy penalty**, **not self-applied Pressure**; the author is considering **replacing** that accuracy penalty with one self-applied PP modeled on Double Strike, but **has not approved that replacement**. No new offhand PP, Pressure effect, injury threshold, headshot execution rule, instant incapacitation, enchantment interaction or extra form modifier is authorized.

**Status:** Headshot eligibility, exposed-versus-gap distinction, +25% head-gap Power, single-target restriction and ordinary multiplicative interaction are **approved**. A self-PP Power Attack redesign remains **a separate proposal** pending evaluation; existing +25% Power Attack with +1 harder hit difficulty stays operative. Other alternative headshot bonuses (15/35/50%) remain noncanon.

## November 16, 2026 — Power Attack +25% APPROVED; headshot Power under study

**Latest explicit author approval:** **Power Attack** is an approved physical maneuver, using the selected **+25% final Attack Power** candidate from the November 16 ordinary-enemy review. It is an **offensive Full Attack costing both actions (2)** under the existing two-action/one-offensive-action limit; resolve its eligible attack **one Naked-d20 hit-difficulty category harder**, then apply **×1.25 to that attack's otherwise legal final physical Attack Power after the normal weapon/attribute/Quality/material calculation**, before the existing covered-AR/END Impact and injury-stage tests. The attack uses its normal lawful physical reach, attack mode, actual coverage/penetration, target defenses and hit routing. It does not provide a second strike, an extra action, a free shield bypass or additional armor penetration. It **cannot be combined with Quick Attack, Double Strike, or another distinct Full Attack in the same turn**. Any shield benefit depends on its existing independently valid normal Guard and bash rules, not a new exemption; no special stacking with spells is created.

**Unapproved alternatives:** The previously explored **+50% Power Attack** and any **headshot/vital-location extra Power multiplier** are **not operative**. Existing legally accessible exposed-region (+1 category, genuine AR0) and protected-joint/gap (+2 categories, actual AR25) targeting remains unchanged, including its real-geometry and armor conditions. The author next requested further comparative headshot testing, not approval of a head-specific damage multiplier or any guaranteed anatomically vulnerable region. Combining approved Power Attack with an already-lawful called shot is not an extra attack: apply each legitimate difficulty shift once, clamp the hit category, and use the target region's actual defense; any future vital bonus and how it interacts must be expressly approved.

**Design status:** Prior diagnostic tests against ordinary and END-specialist enemies motivate further examination; they do not change wound threshold, stage cap, physical weapon Fs, proposed independent Weapon Ratings, offense/defense attributes, or unlock a new proficiency tree. No mandatory ongoing AI combat simulation or playtest schedule.

## November 15, 2026 — Shield Deflect stance RETIRED (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**Latest explicit author correction:** **Deflect is removed from the playable Mystaria combat rules, including the current physical tabletop pilot.** It is vestigial and is **not** an available shield stance, action, reaction, alternative Guard mode, or shield-specific one-Pressure-Point counteraction. A shieldbearer may use legal **Guard**, **ordinary shield bash**, or **Light/Medium shield-and-weapon Double Strike** (Heavy shield Double Strike remains prohibited), plus other independently approved actions and prepared **Parry** under their actual rules. **Ordinary offhand Defense with a genuinely usable offhand weapon remains intact** and is *not* renamed, deleted, or granted for free merely by holding a shield. All older passages offering or approving shield **Deflect** in this document or other canon files are **historical and superseded**; historical descriptions are preserved solely to show design evolution. Nothing else about Shield Rating, physical contact routing, durability, magical Guard, Pressure, Double Strike, Parry, Dash or movement is changed. Future balancing questions no longer include whether to keep Deflect.

## November 15, 2026 — Two-action combat and Candidate A shield rules APPROVED FOR PHYSICAL TABLETOP PILOT

**Latest author approval and scope:** Use the reviewed combat recommendations as an **author-approved tabletop pilot**, to be played by the author with friends at their own pace, **not as an AI-run playtest or scheduled balancing program**. These pilot procedures take precedence over inconsistent earlier physical combat/Guard and evasion-cutoff text below, but do not certify permanent balance, alter existing equipment Power, END, AR, injury thresholds, material abilities, SP/MP costs, crafting, racial exceptions, or authorize new magic resolution. No follow-up or play log is required unless the author chooses to discuss observations.

### Pilot action economy, hit dice and Speed

- **Two actions per combatant turn, maximum one offensive attack action per turn.** **Full Attack (2 actions)** uses the ordinary full legal Power and hit difficulty. **Quick Attack (1 action)** uses **full otherwise final attack Power**, with hit difficulty **one category harder**; the old 80%-Power Quick test is not the pilot rule. The unused action can support a different legal one-action choice, but never a second attack.
- **Double Strike is a Full Attack (2 actions)**. Retain **two independent attacks at 60% of each attack's otherwise final Power**, one user self-PP, one legal target per hand, existing offhand/Guard sacrifices and existing Light/Medium shield eligibility; **Heavy shields cannot Double Strike**. It is not a second offensive action, nor an extra Quick Attack.
- **Naked d20 categories:** Certain = automatic success; Easy = **4+**; Average = **8+**; Hard = **12+**; Very Hard = **17+**; Impossible = no ordinary roll. Resolve genuine contextual difficulty shifts then clamp to these six categories. Hitting still requires the ordinary Power/Defense check to cause an injury.
- Keep the approved **Combat Speed = effective SPD + effective DEX/4**, with actual existing armor SPD modifier applied to the combined result, and legitimate separate wound/equipment effects. For this pilot, compare defender/attacker Combat Speed using these **wider cutoffs**: ratio below **0.20** Certain; **0.20–<0.80** Easy; **0.80–<1.25** Average; **1.25–<2.50** Hard; **2.50–<5.00** Very Hard; **5.00+** Impossible. Equality enters the harder band. The former **0.50/0.80/1.25/1.50/3.00** boundaries remain a later human-play comparison, not an additional modifier. This does not change initiative or actual physical movement distance.

### Candidate A physical shield Guard (PILOT)

A real, usable, stat-qualified shield in **Guard**, actually capable of defending the selected physical line, shifts hit difficulty **one category harder with Light/Medium** or **two harder with Heavy**. Apply this single combined shield shift **instead of** the previous automatic Guard interception and separate +0/+1/+1 shield-body-line obstruction for that same attack. Do **not** double count shield coverage. An undefendable line receives no shield modifier.

**Use one natural d20**, checking both the **baseline category** (all lawful non-shield modifiers) and **shield-shifted category**. Fails baseline = **natural miss, no shield contact or wear**. Passes baseline but fails shielded check = **actual shield contact**, using existing shield Defense, material, condition loss and only-on-break torso overflow. Passes both = **body hit**, resolved with actual END, armor and wound rules. Certain/Impossible endpoints retain their defined meaning. Example, an ordinary Average line: Light or Medium gives **35% miss / 20% shield / 45% body**; Heavy **35% miss / 45% shield / 20% body**. Those percentages describe **routing**, not wound probabilities.

Existing geometry, real simultaneous opposing lines, legal initiative coordination and **net 3+ PP Extreme Pressure** govern whether the shield can defend a line. The old automatic-Guard-only one-of-two flank-interception procedure is not the pilot routing; flanking alone does **not** grant a free hit or universal bypass. Preserve actual existing Shield Rating, durability and overflow; Junk/ceramic/Bellcopper/Thornspring rules; and **separate eligible Glassweave/Nullporcelain magical spell interception** without inventing spell absorption values.

**Shield Deflect has been explicitly retired.** A shield does not grant the ordinary offhand Defense PP cancellation; that independent mechanic remains available only through otherwise lawful offhand use. Candidate A Guard, shield bash and Light/Medium shield Double Strike remain as written.

### Parry and Dash (PILOT)

- **Half Parry:** **1 action**, makes the eligible incoming attack **+1 category harder**, earns **1 PP** if Parry really prevents a would-be body hit. **Full Parry:** **2 actions**, **+2 categories**, earns **2 PP** on such prevention. Prepare one specific outcome-independent eligible trigger on the parrying character's own turn. Use the **same attack d20** and resolve actual physical shield contact before Parry. An ordinary natural miss or shield contact cannot generate free Parry PP. A credible natural miss may be deliberately narrated as intercepted but does not generate PP under this pilot; revisit whether this accounting feels right in player-led play.
- Earned Parry PP persists through the parrying combatant's next turn and refreshes rather than freely stacking from the same source; there is **no automatic counterattack**. A successful **spent Half Parry** prevents new Parry preparation on the next turn; unused preparations expire at the start of that turn without a lock; a successful **Full Parry** waives the cooldown. Full Parry affects one eligible incoming Double Strike strike, not both. Retain independent existing Pressure sources and restrictions.
- **Half Dash / Dash (1 action):** movement allowance **ceil(M × 1.5) tiles** (+50%); retain **+25% Combat Speed** until the start of the user's next turn. **Full Dash / Double Dash (2 actions):** movement allowance **ceil(M × 2) tiles** (+100%); retain **+50% Combat Speed** for that interval. **M** is the ordinary movement-tile allowance determined *before* Dash, not a value recalculated from the temporary Combat Speed increase. **Round fractional tile results UP once, after multiplying.** The temporary Combat Speed bonuses do not themselves generate additional movement tiles. Neither maneuver creates extra attacks, changes initiative, teleports the character or bypasses physical obstacles, positioning/reach control, or other legal restrictions. Ordinary movement-tile baseline and SPD thresholds remain unapproved.

### FUTURE BALANCING QUESTIONS — author-led play, no AI tests or deadlines

The author will simply play with friends and may mention impressions or examples whenever convenient. **Do not schedule, solicit reports, invent playtest outcomes, or run AI encounter tests in response to this backlog.** Questions to revisit only if the author raises them:

1. **Low-level progress:** Do level-1 covered fights stall because actual starting attacks cannot wound, or because contacts are merely infrequent? Does the approved DM wound-route check provide a real alternative?
2. **High-END damage limits:** Can otherwise legal attacks wound even without a shield, including the extreme AR-0 threshold cases? Avoid treating every nonprogression case as an accuracy problem.
3. **Shields:** Are the three classes defensively distinct without interminable Heavy-shield mirrors? Does fewer shield contacts make physical wear, ceramic shatter, Bellcopper recharge and Thornspring Recoil too rare?
4. **Attack choices:** Is full-Power/harder-category Quick Attack useful but not dominant, especially with Half Dash? Do Double Strike and high-grade offhands remain worthwhile when both 60%-Power hits clear injury thresholds?
5. **Defensive behavior:** Do Quick + Half Parry or sustained Full Parry slow combat? Are prepared triggers, result-aware PP, cooldown and no-reward-on-miss intuitively satisfying?
6. **Speed:** Do wider **0.20/0.80/1.25/2.50/5.00** cutoffs preserve meaningful Speed specialization compared with the prior **0.50/0.80/1.25/1.50/3.00** cutoffs?
7. **Positioning:** Do Dash, pursuit, withdrawal, reach, obstacles, movement geometry, multiple attackers and explicit objectives support interesting choices? What timing ambiguity arises at the table?
8. **Shield flanking:** Are opposing simultaneous lines clear and fair under Candidate A, with no Deflect stance?
9. **Magic integration:** Magical Parry, mixed physical/casting actions, reactive spell timing, power-to-shield-absorption, partial spell effects and magical ceramic wear remain independently unresolved. Do not assume new spell mechanics from this physical pilot.
10. **Ease and enjoyment:** Can players resolve one-die miss/shield/body/Parry routing quickly, and do their actual choices feel consequential?

**Evidence scope:** The November 15 integrated shield reproduction archive is experimental evidence, not new magic canon or an automatically committed GitHub dataset. Its sample findings motivate these questions but do not impose any AI follow-up.

## November 15, 2026 — Encounter wound-route viability; mandatory for ChatGPT-run Mystaria (AUTHOR APPROVED)

**Approved DM encounter-design requirement:** Whenever a Hero is expected to overcome an opponent by inflicting wounds, the DM must verify **before presenting or committing to the encounter** that the Hero has **at least one genuinely plausible way to wound that opponent**, based on the Hero's **actual usable equipment, abilities, stat/Power, the defender's END and armor, physical reach and clearance, and the battlefield**. Do not assume an attack can wound merely because it can hit. Apply the established Power/Defense calculation and the **strict Impact >0.75 wound threshold**, including the correct physical armor bypass and any physically available protection coverage.

**Especially important when Mystaria is played through ChatGPT:** The assistant acting as DM **must perform this wound-route check as part of encounter preparation**, particularly for early-level, high-END, heavily armored or shielded enemies. Do not create a compulsory ordinary fight where every legal attack available to the Hero is incapable of inflicting a wound. A theoretical alternative weapon not actually owned or accessible, an impossible hit category, an unavailable spell or attack, a Sweep without enough space, or an arbitrarily invented uncovered weak spot **does not** establish a plausible wound route. Genuine exposed regions, protected joints, rear attacks, appropriate weapon modes and other already legal tactics count only where the geometry, coverage, hit legitimacy and current character capabilities permit them. Armor bypass cannot solve a case where even a genuine **AR 0** contact remains below wound threshold.

**DM remedies if the check fails:** Change the foe to one with lower effective END/protection, furnish a legitimate achievable tactical opening, make appropriate usable equipment/assistance available through normal fiction, or design a clearly feasible **non-wounding objective** (withdrawal, escape, negotiation, environmental goal, rescue, containment or similar). A deliberate overwhelming threat, survival or escape scene is allowed **if the intended player objective does not require wounding that enemy**. Do not make every opponent automatically vulnerable or guarantee the Hero victory; challenge, risk, and failure remain possible, but the intended victory condition must have an achievable route.

**Scope:** This is encounter preparation and ChatGPT DM operating guidance, not a change to enemy stats, approved armor AR, weapon F, hit probabilities, injury stages, starting gear, level advancement, or an automatic targeting action. Motivating evidence: the November 15 early equipment audit reproduced the level-1 Drider Hand Axe stalemate and identified some high-END matchups still below threshold even with AR 0. The detailed audit findings are evidence, not independent new combat canon.

---

## November 14, 2026 — Prepared magical defense and mixed-action combat

On their own turn, a caster allocates their shared Channel Limit to any offensive magic and at most **one specific prepared reactive defensive spell**. Other applicable channeling, including medium recovery and contests, draws from the same limit. The caster declares a narrow trigger in advance, such as the next attack against a specified target or by a specified attacker. A generic watch for attacks on any ally and triggers based on whether an attack would hit are invalid. When the trigger occurs, the spell may be activated at a legitimate response opportunity using the capacity reserved on the caster's own turn, with the required physical medium and access and at its normal MP cost. Borrowing from the next turn's Channel Limit is prohibited. The prepared defense expires at the **beginning of the caster’s next turn**, whether used or unused. Protection beyond that point requires casting a separate spell with sufficient duration or eligible maintenance.

Normal physical actions may be combined with otherwise legal magic, but the applicable Channel Limit is **halved and rounded down for that mixed turn**, and no more than **one attack total** may be made. No independent extra attack/action is granted; physical Double Strike cannot be layered into a hybrid two-attack turn. Exact reaction timing, action priority and interactions with specialist rules are pending adjudication. Existing Wyrmcraft exceptions, Warlock handling, physical Shield Guard, preexisting ordinary combat actions and already-approved spells remain otherwise operative. Full philosophy: [Magical Media and Contests](35_Magical_Media_and_Contests.md).

**Bloomkin crossbow and prepared shield:** A Bloomkin may fire a crossbow as their one attack and prepare an eligible magical shield for a nearby Thornguard. “The next attack targeting my knight” is a valid trigger. “The next attack that hits my knight” depends on the outcome and is invalid.

**Thornguard sword and shield:** A Thornguard may make one normal sword attack and retain eligible physical shield Guard with a qualified, usable shield. The interception follows ordinary Guard rules, including the specific ceramic shield exception below where applicable. Thornguard cannot personally shape mana or cast a reactive shield spell. Whether a separate eligible caster or a lawfully powered device can provide a magical shield in this example remains conditional on an established ability or interface and its own rules.

---

## November 13, 2026 — Specific magical shield interception exception and material Guard interactions (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**Spell interception exception:** A usable, qualified Nullporcelain or Glassweave shield in Guard can intercept **any incoming spell effect against its bearer**, including psychic effects and the bearer's exposure to an area spell. Resolve the shield's absorption against that exposure. When the shield absorbs the entire incoming spell Power, the bearer is unaffected. When spell Power exceeds what the shield can absorb, the excess reaches the bearer and the spell resolves against them under its normal rules. An area interception protects only that bearer; the area and its other targets resolve normally. If Guard is unavailable under existing rules, including net Extreme Pressure of at least 3 PP, the spell resolves against the bearer normally. Against two genuine coordinated simultaneous flankers, the existing one-interception limit applies; the unblocked attack resolves normally. Apply ×3 Nullporcelain or ×1.75 Glassweave to physical Shield Rating before squaring for magical Shield Defense. Spell magnitude-to-Power conversion, the exact absorption and overflow calculation, partial-effect adjudication for non-damage spells, and whether magical contact causes ceramic wounds remain unresolved. Balanced B physical Rating/support STR factors are ×1.15/×0.75 Nullporcelain and ×1.25/×0.95 Glassweave.

**Additional authored defensive/offensive identities (detail in [Shield Material Profiles](29_Physical_Shield_Profiles.md)):** Skyvein returning shield throw takes one normal attack action, can reach up to **Light1/Medium2/Heavy3** distinct targets and sacrifices **every shield benefit for the thrower's whole turn**; no early-return Guard. Reprise Bronze prepared shield forms switch at turn start with no action, using that class's handling and attack options with one fixed Medium-based Rating. Ready **Bellcopper** multiplies final physical shield Defense by **×1.50 Medium / ×2 Heavy**. Actual condition loss after enhanced Defense charges the reservoir, disabling the multiplier until **three uninterrupted qualifying quiet turns** pass. Light shield contact is acoustically muffled. Thornspring shield melee-block Recoil applies **one opposing PP for one turn** by spending one Springload, with a capacity of one. Hearthiron shield itself has **×3 Fire resistance** and approved **10%/20%/35%** Fire-effect budgets for Light/Medium/Heavy bashes, with elemental conversion and heat costs unresolved. All other approved Guard/Deflect/Double Strike Pressure tiers, injury, blocking, shield-only break overflow and attack-evasion rules remain unchanged.

---

## November 13, 2026 — Automatic shield interception, ceramic immediate shatter, Junk shielding AUTHOR APPROVED (LATEST)

**CURRENT SHIELD COMBAT OVERRIDE:** When a character is equipped with a usable, stat-qualified shield and in **Guard**, with **net Pressure below Extreme Pressure (net <3 PP)**, any otherwise successful **physically blockable attack** against that bearer is **AUTOMATICALLY intercepted by the shield**. This includes physically interceptable ranged shots. No extra block roll, awareness/reflex/projectile timing check, facing selection or reaction bookkeeping is needed. The ordinary attack still checks hit/evasion normally; a miss is not a free shield contact. The shield automatically covers the Guard wielder against ordinary non-synchronized physical attacks. Actual shield contact resolves once against shield Defense and durability, not a second body hit, except pre-existing breaking-blow torso overflow. Nonphysical/spell/psychic attack protection is not newly invented.

**Specific multi-opponent exception:** Two legal attackers **physically flanking** the defender, with **adjacent initiative entries**, may elect to attack at the same time using their own normal attack actions, rather than resolving separate automatically intercepted strikes. The shieldbearer chooses one of their simultaneous incoming attacks to intercept; the other resolves its normal legal hit/evasion/body defenses **without shield interception**. It is not automatically successful or unarmored. Voluntary ally initiative delay for credible coordinated attack, separately approved before this ruling, remains legal under its own timing/prerequisites; this simple adjacent-initiative option requires no extra delay or new reaction-point resource. An actual Shield already Broken, a shield not wielded in Guard, or a defender in **Extreme Pressure net ≥3 PP** loses normal automatic deliberate interception. Deflect and Light/Medium shield Double Strike continue to forfeit active block by their own approved stance design; actual coincidental physical contact remains possible. The larger Heavy face and conditional Body-line obstruction +0/+1/+1 do not create another automatic cover roll atop the same intercepted contact.

**Ceramic shield condition override:** Any otherwise legally constructed genuinely ceramic load-bearing shield **shatters immediately on its FIRST physical contact that would cause any ordinary shield wear**: `P ≥ D`, `D=Rating²`. No Dented/Cracked ceramic shield stage. Contacts below D do not wound or shatter it. Apply approved torso breaking overflow once with the intact ceramic capacity `R=3`: `max(0,P−3D)`. This replaces the previous Work shield ceramic 0.75D/1.75D wear bands. The author further requires a comparatively high **defensive strength-to-STR-minimum** for ceramic shields; final Nullporcelain and Glassweave defensive Rating/STR factors are **OPEN pending dedicated calibration**. Older optional ×0.35 and ×1.00 ceramic Rating factors do not independently satisfy this new target and are not final manufacturable product stats. Existing actual ceramic ARMOR shatter triggers remain unchanged.

**Junk shields:** Junk remains QI Boughsteel with approved half-QI-price rule; physical Shield Defense **=90%** of equivalent ordinary shield's `Rating²`, NOT 90% of Rating before the square. **Two usable states only** `Intact→Dented→Broken`, break on second loss rather than third, keep full functionality while Dented. Standard contact-loss bracket comparison uses that reduced defense; breaking overflow uses `R=2−prior accumulated losses` and this Junk-specific D. Do NOT add a Junk weapon/armor hit shift without actual supporting approved shield rule.

**Smith repair and physical ammo:** Any smith capable of crafting the particular repairable item spends **50% of its actual otherwise lawful craft time** to repair it completely; existing material, item and exceptional craft requirements remain. Ordinary weapon repair 4/8/12h and armor repair 16/20/28h from established base manufacture hours, subject to legitimate production variations. Shield manufacture work-hours are still not approved and therefore shield repair has no finalized absolute hours. Ammunition **cannot be repaired except actual throwing weapons**. Costs/components/other shop operation remain in the **mundane crafting/economy backlog**.

**Proficiency backlog, not free targeted attacks:** develop called shots to a wielding hand, shield straps and genuine equipment weak points later; preserve existing ordinary exposed-body and armor-gap attack categories. **Complete statistical end-to-end combat win-rate balance is deferred until AFTER enchantments are designed.** No actual hit probabilities or special material shield/armor abilities have been invented here. Full implementation, conflicts and current scope in [29_Physical_Shield_Profiles.md](29_Physical_Shield_Profiles.md).

---

## November 13, 2026 — AUTHOR APPROVED physical shield Rating, eligibility, blocking and material baseline (LATEST)

**Approval:** The author instructed us to follow Work's shield decision report recommendations based on `main` commit `57e1632`. Approved **Boughsteel shield Ratings** Quality I–V are Light **18/32/53/78/105**, Medium **24/43/71/104/140**, Heavy **31/55/90/135/180**. Approved QI Boughsteel minimum **STR/DEX:** Light **10/12**, Medium **27/14**, Heavy **62/16** before existing Quality and Equipment Load Synergy. Other previously approved 75% equipment eligibility, largest-shortfall-only SPD penalty, armor movement, actual combat speed, and zero inherent shield SPD remain intact. Approved **material defensive Rating/support-STR** factors Boughsteel **1/1**, Trufula **0.90/0.60**, Adamantite **1.15/2.50**, with shield Rating rounded nearest whole halves up before squaring, and handling STR/DEX rounded UP once after respective relevant modifier chains. The shield's **physical `D = Rating²`** excludes wearer's END and body AR, and ordinary physical penetration does not diminish shield D.

**Approved numeric shield-contact injury and remaining-capacity interaction:** On a genuine physically intercepted hit `P`, ordinary shield wear is **0 if P<D; 1 if D≤P<2D; 2 if 2D≤P<3D; 3 if P≥3D**. Keep three ordinary losses Intact→Dented→Cracked→Broken, with full function before Broken. Cap losses at the remaining track. **No torso overflow until the hit newly breaks shield**. If it breaks, `R = 3 − prior losses`, and existing **`P_torso=max(0,P−R×D)`** proceeds against actual torso END/AR and real attack armor-bypass once. Do NOT double-hit body or stack Power between distinct Double Strike strikes.

**Approved Guard geometry:** Light gets **+0**, Medium **+1**, Heavy **+1** hit-difficulty category only if a shield actually obstructs an otherwise open *body-target* line in eligible **Guard**. Heavy's larger physical face may interrupt more real attack lines but has **no stronger numeric category shift** than Medium. Intercepted shield contacts resolve shield D rather than also body-line obstruction; actual flanks, gaps, limbs, cover, awareness and geometry determine which defense is legal. No universal passive full-body +1 category, fixed facing arc or double-counted shield-as-cover bonus.

**Approved existing tactics reaffirmed:** All shields retain Guard, Deflect and one single full `STR F1.05` Narrow Bludgeoning bash with legal active Guard block; Light/Medium shield+weapon Double Strike makes two independently legal `0.60×final Power` attacks with existing one self-PP and surrendered block/obstruction/Deflect; Heavy shield cannot Double Strike. **No new per-Quality or per-material shield-bash F modifier** is granted by defensive Rating alone. Net 3+ PP and real simultaneous opposite-angle threats continue to limit active shields.

**Ceramic exception and special materials:** For any **independently legal** shield with a real ceramic load-bearing face, authorize shield-only wear 0 at `P≤0.75D`, 2 at `0.75D<P≤1.75D`, 3 at `P>1.75D`. Preserve existing full protection until shattered; break overflow still floors `max(0,P−R×D)`. This does NOT amend separately approved ceramic *armor* injury-triggered breakage or manufacture unsupported new shields. Optional special-material shield Rating/STR coefficients and valid face/frame examples are detailed in [Physical Shield Profiles](29_Physical_Shield_Profiles.md); they are conditional references only for genuinely allowable constructions, not new recipes or equipped suit abilities. The historical flat `S=30/50/75`, `QV=160/225/270`, obstruction `+1/+1/+2` and older STR/DEX gates are **SUPERSEDED** for operative shield numbers.

**Full physical item tables and limits:** [29_Physical_Shield_Profiles.md](29_Physical_Shield_Profiles.md) (class/quality material tables, squared-Power thresholds, support prerequisites, ceramics, positioning, scope limitations, open issues). The Work report's outcomes are conditional on attempted physical contacts and benchmark assumptions, not new universal hit rates or full-combat win odds. Exact projectile reactive timing, direct strap/limb targeting, Junk shields, special repair and manufactured forms remain open.

## November 13, 2026 — Shield category tactical restriction and zero inherent shield SPD burden APPROVED (LATEST)

**Newest explicit author approval (supersedes only shield Double Strike eligibility and previously proposed shield mobility):** **Light and Medium shields may use the already approved weapon-plus-shield Double Strike**, provided all ordinary real reach/geometry, qualification and action conditions are met. **A Heavy shield CANNOT be used for Double Strike**, including weapon-plus-shield Double Strike or a proposed alternate two-hit technique with the Heavy shield. The Heavy shield remains usable for an ordinary **single shield bash** (approved STR, Bludgeoning Narrow, F1.05) as the attack action **while retaining otherwise eligible active blocking**. Heavy shield also retains Guard, qualifying Deflect (normal offhand Defense PP counteraction instead of block/obstruction), and its actual physically positioned shield defense. The approved ordinary two-weapon Double Strike remains available to qualified combatants wielding **two actual weapons** rather than a Heavy shield; do not generally ban that maneuver for Heavy-armored fighters.

**No inherent SPD penalty for any shield class**, Light, Medium or Heavy. The formerly tested −1%, −3%, −5%, −10%, −15% or any separate inherent shield SPD surcharge were **never approved and now expressly rejected**. Heavy and other body armor retain their independently approved material/coverage SPD modifiers. **Actual shield handling still matters:** apply existing author-approved shield STR/DEX minimum eligibility, Equipment Load Synergy and **single-largest STR/DEX item-shortfall SPD penalty** when relevant. Zero intrinsic shield burden does NOT exempt an underqualified shield wielder from the approved handling shortfall penalty. Any inherent armor burden and legitimate shortfall continue to affect actual SPD, initiative and ordinary Combat Speed under their owning approved rules; no new additive-versus-multiplicative stacking rule is needed for an intrinsic shield penalty of zero.

**Design intent:** Heavy shield physical interception, higher STR handling requirements, broad real physical coverage and the inability to Double Strike create its offensive/defensive specialization tradeoff. A Medium shield provides normal shield defense plus optional 60%-Power sword-and-bash Double Strike with the usual sacrificed block and one self-PP; a Light shield preserves mobility and analogous legal tactics. **Do not treat earlier experimental shield Ratings (including proposed QV Light160/Medium225/Heavy270), obstruction categories (+1,+1,+2 or 0,+1,+1), material Rating multipliers, STR/DEX shield gates, durability-loss calibration bands or special combat hit percentages as newly approved.** Only the Heavy Double Strike restriction and zero inherent shield mobility burden are new canon in this entry. All prior valid shield block, actual collision, only-on-break torso overflow, Deflect and pressure constraints remain.

## November 13, 2026 — Double Strike, shield bashes and shield tactical commitments AUTHOR APPROVED (LATEST OPERATIVE COMBAT CANON)

**Latest explicit author approval:** The author approved the complete immediately preceding **60%-Power Double Strike, F1.05 shield bash, shield Guard / Deflect / Double Strike** recommendation, including its simple offensive self-Pressure tradeoff and one target per hand. **Intent clarified by author:** the former **offhand-investment problem** was not that daggers are unfairly strong. Under the original Attack/Defense choice the **offhand's weapon Power, material, Quality and installed enchantments provided little-to-no benefit to that tactical choice**, so a plain Quality I dagger often competed with an extravagant enhanced secondary weapon. **Double Strike makes both weapons' real offensive specifications matter by providing an optional separately resolved hit for the offhand.** There is NO requirement that an offhand's F, damage, rarity, Quality, or Gem value match the main hand, and ordinary daggers remain useful.

### Approved Double Strike: two independent attacks, reduced final Power

1. **One normal attack action**, with an actual separately equipped, legally usable main-hand weapon and offhand weapon. Make **two separately resolved ordinary melee attack attempts**, one per weapon, against one selected opponent or two distinct opponents if actually reachable and physically engageable. Apply the existing Combat Speed/evasion comparison, real cover/geometry, attack mode and target defenses separately. The second contact is NOT guaranteed and still needs a legitimate target.
2. Each weapon computes its own **normal completed physical attack Power** using its **actual lawful attack attribute, APPROVED individual F, material and Quality, and any legitimately applicable installed enchantment effect**. **Double Strike final physical Power for EACH hit = 0.60 × that weapon's otherwise applicable final attack Power.** This is **AFTER** squaring its base attack-rating `(A×F)²`; multiplying F or A by 0.60 **before** the square is incorrect (it would leave 36% Power). Each hit independently uses its real Slashing/Piercing/Blunt armor bypass, END/covered AR, injury stages and relevant Shimmer Guard. Enchantments only contribute when actually operative under their existing rules, with no new free proc, charge, spell or second application to an unrelated weapon.
3. **Each hand may threaten ONE valid target in Double Strike.** The hands may each threaten the same legal enemy, or two different legal enemies; Double Strike never turns each separately rolled attack into a free Sweep/Wide Sweep/Line that targets 2–3 enemies or grants extra group-casualty aggregate Power. The ordinary single-weapon multi-target forms remain available when using a normal attack instead of Double Strike. Normal target reach, real clearance and obstructed attack lines remain.
4. While committed to Double Strike, the user **cannot also choose the regular offhand Attack or Defense tactical role**: no offhand offensive **+1 Pressure Point against the defender**, no normal offhand defensive Pressure cancellation, and no same-user tactical double counting with Suppressive Fire/Concentrated Burst. The two Power-reduced physical blows are the whole offensive technique; they do NOT automatically impose Pressure on targets.
5. **The Double Strike USER acquires exactly ONE self-applied meaningful Pressure Point** for reckless commitment, **from initiating the attack until the start of that user's next turn**. This is one source **total**, not one per weapon, per connected hit or per target. Apply the current established net Pressure framework: 1–2 meaningful net PP = incoming hit difficulty one category easier, 3+ = two categories easier, subject to actually credible incoming attacks and independent valid counter-pressure. No separate generic evasion penalty, extra damage susceptibility, automatic reaction attack or new status counter is invented.

**Normal offhand Attack OR Defense remains available instead of Double Strike.** A character may choose the older useful credible offensive +1 PP or defensive response, but those modes still do not generate an automatic extra physical attack or attach an offhand weapon's raw Power to a mere Pressure contribution. The author explicitly wants stronger offhand equipment to have a concrete optional payoff through Double Strike while preserving the cheap dagger's independent Piercing, Short reach, grapple, material and low-price virtues.

### Approved shield combat tactics

A qualified character holding a real usable shield may choose a compatible **Guard**, **Deflect**, or **Double Strike** commitment for the relevant attack/exchange; physical positions, already-approved support prerequisites, relevant conditions and the previously approved net-3+ Extreme Pressure restriction still apply.

**Guard / normal single attack:** Make **ONE ordinary weapon attack OR ONE ordinary shield bash** as the attack action. The bearer retains otherwise valid deliberate shield interception/block, existing shield obstruction/body-hit geometry, actual shield durability and only-on-break torso overflow. **Choosing a single shield bash does not itself forfeit an eligible active block**; but it does NOT grant a bonus free attack on blocking. The same shield cannot intercept simultaneous opposite-direction threats, and an otherwise ineligible block stays ineligible.

**Shield Bash physical attack (APPROVED):** Ordinary one-hit **STR-led, Bludgeoning, Narrow coverage, F = 1.05**, using the same normal physical Power formula `(effective STR×1.05×applicable legitimate material/Quality adjustments, if any)²` with no made-up automatic extra factor. Apply the established ordinary Bludgeoning **25% covered-AR bypass** as applicable to its actual contact form. The ordinary one-handed mace primary F1.17 remains superior in equal-attribute attack Power: shield bash is `(1.05/1.17)² ≈ 80.5%` of that mace's raw Power on equal effective STR. The item must be physically wieldable and capable of delivering a bash. The approved numerical bash F is a standard base profile; **specific shield material interactions, shape-specific handling minima and shield Defense Rating tables remain separate/open**, not inferred from this F. No separate free shield-bash action is created.

**Deflect / offhand Defense:** Choose to **give up intentional active blocking/parrying AND the shield's deliberate body-hit obstruction/deflection hit-difficulty bonus** for the committed exchange. In return, the held shield takes the existing offhand **Defense** tactical role: when an incoming adversary's Pressure is credible and physically addressable, a suitably qualified shield user can **counteract/remove ONE applicable incoming PP** through actual efficient deflection, following the normal nonautomatic offhand Defense rules. It is NOT an unconditional 1-PP permanent immunity, an extra evasion category, the ordinary offhand Attack PP or a second free defense on one motion. The shield's actual already-present material can still be struck by literal incidental contact. Deflect does NOT grant a deliberate shield durability-backed interception.

**Weapon + Shield Double Strike (Light or Medium shields only; Heavy shield forbidden by latest approval above):** The main-hand weapon and **one legal Light/Medium shield bash** each resolve as their own attack, each at **60% of its own final Power**. The user loses **all deliberate active blocks, intentional shield-body obstruction, and offhand Defense/Attack tactical modes** while so committed, and takes the exact **one self-imposed PP** until the next turn. The shield is not able to actively intercept incoming hits in this stance; real accidental impacts against its positioned physical surface are still possible but are not a deliberate block. Normal single shield bash under Guard is the alternative that retains legal active blocking.

**Boundary / previous documents:** The immediately following earlier November 13 `Double Strike / shield action balance study (DRAFT)` documents the numerical tests and rejected candidate percentages as HISTORICAL DRAFT ANALYSIS. Its recommended 60%, one-target-per-hand, F1.05, one self-PP and tactical shield mode tradeoffs are **NOW EXPRESSLY APPROVED above**. Older absolute "offhand never makes a second physical attack" provisions are **superseded ONLY when the approved Double Strike action is used**. Shield obstruction numbers Buckler/Standard/Tower **+1/+1/+2**, shield Ratings **30/50/75**, shield attribute gates and numerical durability-loss impact bands remain **CALIBRATION CANDIDATES, NOT NOW APPROVED**. The shield's existing independently approved durability states, remaining-capacity overflow equation, body armor, wearer's stat shortfall, legitimate facing and Extreme Pressure constraints remain unchanged. No automatic hit probabilities or completed fight win rates are authorized by the deterministic wound-stage tests.

---

## November 13, 2026 — HISTORICAL Double Strike / shield balance study (draft at time of testing; SELECTED VALUES NOW APPROVED ABOVE)

**The author requested proposal and testing, NOT approval of a new combat action or exact coefficient.** Existing offhand Attack (credible +1 PP against target) OR Defense (credible counteraction to hostile PP), actual shield intercept/durability, hit difficulty and no-free-double-attack canon remain in force until expressly changed.

### Numerical Double Strike candidate: each of TWO hits uses 60% of its own FINAL Power

**PROPOSED, NOT CANON:** Spend one normal attack action attempting one attack per actually equipped and qualified hand. For each weapon compute its lawful normal STR/DEX/Either/Both attack Power with approved actual F, damage form, material/Quality, armor bypass, then **multiply FINAL Power ×0.60 once** and separately resolve its hit/evasion and physical END/AR for its own target. Never multiply the pre-square rating or F by 0.60, which would produce only 36% of Power. Choose the same target twice only if still a lawful target after the first impact, or choose two different actually reachable foes. **One target per weapon, no combined Sweep/Wide Sweep/Line multi-target multiplication in this proposed mode**, avoiding four or six group victims from one action. Normal single-weapon Sweep/Wide Sweep/Line remains unchanged outside this candidate action.

**Exclusivity/risk, proposed:** Double Strike forfeits ordinary offhand Attack pressure and offhand Defense PP counteraction for the exchange, makes two separate attacks but does **NOT** automatically grant +1 PP against foes, and causes **one self-applied incoming PP** for reckless exposure (never one per hand or target), using existing pressure tier rules on eligible enemies. Simple proposed duration **until start of striker's next turn**, not yet approved. Each incoming credible enemy attack may benefit from the resulting legitimate pressured state; do not assume arbitrary accuracy probabilities.

### Proposed shield tactical menu, NOT yet canon

**Guard and one Shield Bash:** Make the usual single weapon attack, or a separately selected **shield bash as the normal attack**, while retaining every *otherwise legal* directional shield block and relevant obstruction. A bash does not generate an automatic follow-up attack on block, nor an extra attack while using the sword. **Proposed ordinary physical bash profile: STR-led F1.05, Bludgeoning Narrow, ordinary 25% covered AR bypass**. The approved one-hand mace is F1.17 Bludgeoning, meaning the proposed bash has about **80.5% of mace raw Power at equal STR**, not 80.5% of F. Exact bash F, form/shape and equipment handling remain pending author approval.

**Deflect:** Sacrifice **active shield interception/blocking** AND any intentional **shield-body hit-difficulty obstruction** for the relevant exchange, gaining the **offhand Defense** mode's one *credible* incoming PP counteraction if actual shield positioning, stats and enemy threat justify it. Not a free unconditional dodge, Shield Rating, or offhand Attack PP. The shield can still accidentally be struck as a real object; this is not a deliberate block. Historical +1/+1/+2 buckler/standard/tower obstruction numbers remain **testing candidates, not final rules**. Deflect may help against multi-threat Pressure or to avoid shield breakage; under normal face-to-face circumstances ordinary Guard may be stronger.

**Weapon + Shield Double Strike:** Same two independently reduced-Power hits, one normal weapon and one lawful Bludgeoning shield bash, taking **one self-PP**, and relinquishing **all active blocks, shield body-obstruction/deflect benefits and offhand tactical choices**. In contrast a single bash can retain eligible active blocking.

### Tests and interpretation, all conditional on actual successful contact

**150 controlled states:** equal attacking weapon attribute 35/50/75/100/150 × defender END20/35/50/75/100 × AR0/25/45/75/115/170. Main and offhand are ordinary F1.14 arming swords with 0% covered-AR bypass; compare two landed reduced hits to one landed normal full-Power sword. Injury-stage thresholds use existing 0.75/1.75/3.00 Impact ratio, 0/1/2/3 stages per hit. Double greater/same/less total stages than one full successful hit: **50%-each 34/88/28; 55% 34/88/28; 60% 44/86/20; 65% 50/84/16; 70% 53/82/15**. The artificial equally weighted 150-state grid has single-hit mean 1.07 injury stages, compared with double-hit means **1.32, 1.33, 1.51, 1.61, 1.65** at respective coefficients. These are **NOT real expected injury per action**: they assume both separate attempted hits connect and omit lost offhand Attack hit-difficulty benefit, self-PP exposure, real spacing and timing.

**Example attack attribute75, target END50, covered AR0/25/45/75/115:** single normal arming-sword Slash yields **2/2/1/1/0** stages; two independent 60%-Power arming-sword Slash hits yield **4/2/2/0/0** if both land. One full proposed STR shield bash F1.05 Blunt yields **2/2/1/1/0**; sword+shield bash double at 60% each yields **3/2/2/0/0**. The new action is powerful versus light protection but can do **no meaningful wound at AR75 where one full committed strike does**, with added exposure and lost shield blocking. A full ordinary two-target sword Sweep can outperform a Double Strike against two covered targets because existing full-Power individual target/Sweep procedures remain independently relevant. The most distinctive multi-target benefit is two separate Point/Narrow strikes threatening separate valid targets.

**Real offhand niches; 11 approved races × levels10/25/50/75/100 × STR/HYBRID/DEX progressions (165 states):** Match same-race END+STR defenders at QI Light AR25, QII Medium AR52, QIII Heavy AR95, QIV Heavy AR105, and QV Adamantite Heavy AR170. Use the correct material/racial stat progression, approved weapon primary F, each weapon's own attack attribute, and *actual* damage form: Arming sword F1.14 Either Slashing0%-bypass; dagger F1.00 DEX Piercing50%; rapier F1.13 DEX Piercing50%; war pick F1.20 STR Piercing50%; proposed shield bash F1.05 STR Blunt25%.
- **STR specialists** (55 race/level cases): two Medium Slash swords outperform sword+DEX dagger conditional stage sum in **32**, tie in **23**, lose in **0**; sword+STR war pick outperforms dagger in **54/55**. A larger offhand creates real offensive value without a minimum offhand Power restriction.
- **DEX specialists** (55 cases): sword+DEX dagger outperforms twin Slashing swords in **14**, ties **41**, loses **0** owing to actual piercing AR bypass; replacing dagger by F1.13 Piercing rapier outperforms dagger in **11/55**, never lowers landed-hit stage and affords its lawful Medium reach, while dagger is cheaper and better inside grapples. Do not remove dagger's own legitimate piercing speciality or invent a blanket bonus for larger weapons.
- **Two legal separate targets with Point weapons:** Two equally qualified DEX75 rapiers F1.13 Piercing vs exposed END35 AR0 opponents: each full normal Point hit has Impact ~5.86, 3 injury stages against **one foe**. Under 60%-Power Double Strike each Point hit has Impact ~3.52, still 3 stages on **each of two distinct foes if both attacks really connect**; unlike a normal Point attack, the proposed action can target two, not obtain a free wider weapon Sweep.

**Shield profile sensitivity, same 150-state grid:** proposed single F1.05 bash and full one-hand F1.17 mace share the same conditional stage in **127/150**, with bash weaker in **23**, never stronger at equal STR and Blunt25% bypass. With sword+shield bash at 60% each, 2-hit stages exceed full single Shield Bash in **37**, fall below in **17**, tie **96**; relative to one ordinary full sword: **43 above / 12 below / 95 equal**. Double does NOT retain block, so raw landed-hit injury stages overstate its combat utility. Alternative F1.08/1.10 make bash even closer to mace and risk reducing the real niche of carrying an actual mace.

**UNAPPROVED RECOMMENDATION:** Start playtesting 60% of each hit's FINAL Power, one physical target per hand, two independent evasion/armor outcomes, one self-PP until next turn, no offhand Attack/Defense combination; test STR F1.05 Blunt Narrow shield bash with legal single-attack blocking, shield Deflect as credible PP counteraction replacing active block and obstruction, and shield+weapon double giving up all shield protection. Do not canonize until the author selects the exact mechanics. All 150 and 165 case comparisons are conditional landed results, not hit percentages or completed fight win rates.

## November 13, 2026 — Ordinary combat accuracy/evasion uses 25% DEX Combat Speed (APPROVED; LATEST)

**Latest author-approved rework:** For ordinary hit/evasion comparisons, **each point of effective DEX contributes one-quarter as much as effective SPD**. The simple rule replaces the older SPD-only comparison while retaining the same six-category ladder:

```text
Combat Speed before armor = effective SPD + effective DEX / 4
Combat Speed after armor  = (effective SPD + effective DEX / 4) × existing armor SPD modifier
Evasion Ratio             = defender current Combat Speed / attacker current Combat Speed
```

Apply any legitimate current wound and equipment-shortfall speed restrictions in accordance with their already approved source procedures, without double counting. Armor's inherent negative or positive SPD percentage affects the **entire combined Combat Speed**, so investing in DEX does not evade armor's burden. Preserve fractional effective Shimmer attributes; no newly imposed rounding or separate arithmetic at each attack, since a character's Combat Speed may be shown as a prepared sheet value.

**Use the original six-category thresholds unchanged** (Certain for ratio <0.50; Easy 0.50–<0.80; Average 0.80–<1.25; Hard 1.25–<1.50; Very Hard 1.50–<3.00; Impossible >=3.00). A Very Hard/Impossible direct attack can still be changed by real existing Pressure, Trapped, geometry, Aim, cover and legal tactical conditions. **No additional DEX category-shift cap, endpoint immunity, special duel condition, accuracy percentage, hit roll, action or resource was approved**; those were rejected more-complicated prior trial suggestions.

**Limited scope:** The derived value is for **ordinary attack accuracy and evasion**, not actual walking/running/sprinting/dashing SPD, movement distances, acceleration, weapon Power, physical Defense or armor AR, attribute requirements, extra attacks, casting, or automatic Pressure. **Initiative remains on current effective SPD**, as already explicitly approved. Similarly, actual-SPD-based reach control or physical movement rules remain in force under their owning provisions. This change leaves previously established material/Quality armor SPD percentages and weapon F unchanged.

**Routine prediction bookkeeping set aside:** Do not require declarations of a predicted move, opponent-DEX-versus-SPD 75% qualification, wrong-prediction 200% exception or an extra prediction-based category shift in every ordinary combat exchange. DEX's routine hit/evasion reward is now included in Combat Speed. Historical narrative predictions and Shimmer Guard's distinct conditional visual-aid feature are not eliminated as story elements or independently approved special abilities, but do not grant a second universal DEX modifier.

**Precedence:** This November 13 ruling supersedes the legacy Evasion Ratio = (defender SPD + 10)/(attacker SPD + 10) from before the global attribute rebase **and** the November 5 SPD-only ratio for ordinary hit/evasion. Do not add a combat-only +10 to already-rebased Shimmer attributes. The user's expected specialization goals remain **design intentions**, not a new deterministic hit rate or guaranteed fight outcome.

**Author's conditional authorization satisfied:** A multi-geometry greatsword/maul/pike battery revealed the working greatsword thrust at **Impact1.707** versus pike **1.798** and its own Slashing **1.408** in the L20 Thornguard B-attacker vs E-defender/QII Full Medium Boughsteel reference. That is too close to dedicated Piercing for an alternate on a strong Wide Sweep weapon, although **it does not prove universal greatsword dominance**. Author wanted about **Impact1.6** if the secondary required tuning. Change **only the Power-side F multiplier** for actual **blade-tip secondary Piercing** from **×0.82 to ×0.80 of that weapon's already-adjusted primary F**, applied **once** before squaring. The tested new greatsword thrust is **Impact1.624** in this exact reference. At fixed A this delivers **64% of primary attack's raw Power**, versus historical 67.24%, a **4.82% reduction from the historical secondary's raw Power**.

**Armor bypass is NOT changed:** All ordinary genuine Piercing, including this secondary, retains **50% covered AR bypass**; qualified earned rear hits can still add **10 percentage points**. Other Bludgeoning/Slashing/Piercing primary Fs, armor AR, END², injury thresholds, hit/evasion difficulty, Shimmer Guard, number of actions and actual targeting restrictions do not change. **Purpose-built secondary spike or beak retains its distinct ×0.90 primary F**, and ordinary non-Piercing alternate attacks keep their respective authorized generic or item-specific factors. The greatsword keeps the shared **×0.875 shape adjustment** once on its eligible primary and **F_secondary=0.80× already adjusted primary** for its Point thrust. Do not count relative form or Quality material factors twice or treat a specific draft individual F as final.

A forward arc Wide Sweep can reach up to three real accessible enemies; Point one; a legal Line up to three straight-path enemies only if not physically blocked. Tight corridors can forbid Wide Sweep, while a pike may legally hit aligned targets. Neither coverage nor squad-only aggregate modifiers grant extra individual Power or guaranteed contact. Established Long-reach grappling and real Pressure still apply. Full stress cases and the draft-individual-F caveat are recorded in `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`.

The earlier 25%-bypass blade thrust and 65%-bypass specialized profile remain retired. This is the latest operative blade-tip source, pending any later author decision.

## November 12, 2026 — Junk attack and worn-armor evasion category penalties (LATEST AUTHOR APPROVAL)

**User-approved Junk-specific attack handling, with exact equipment scope owned by `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`:** A valid attack **made with a Junk Boughsteel QI weapon** is **one category harder to land** on the already-approved six-tier evasion/hit difficulty ladder. A valid ordinary-evasion-resolved attack **made against the wearer of a Junk Boughsteel QI armor ensemble** is **one category easier for the attacker to land**. Both modifications refer to **the landed-hit/evasion difficulty**, NOT impact Power, weapon F, material effects, END, covered AR/MR, body Defense Power, effective SPD/DEX stats, pressure generation, firing/reload timing or additional attack actions.

**Examples:** Baseline Average ⇒ Hard for Junk weapon alone; ⇒ Easy for Junk armor on target alone; ⇒ Average for Junk weapon against Junk-armored defender (opposing +1 harder and −1 easier cancel). Baseline Very Hard ⇒ Impossible if the weapon alone is Junk; if both weapon and armor are Junk, it remains Very Hard. Apply one shift per applicable equipment role/attack, not one for each extra Junk armor accessory, weapon component or overlap. Combine valid step shifts once before applying the six-tier Certain/Impossible boundary. Existing pressure, Aim, cover, range and genuine restrictions/obstructions remain in force. The attack must still be a lawful valid attack and an available evasion contest; a completely unavoidable impact or effect without an ordinary hit-resolution step does not acquire a new dodge chance/damage bonus from Junk armor. These shifts are categorical, **not numerical percentage hit chances**.

**Other Junk effects are economic/enchanted equipment, not combat changes here:** Junk accessories have 50% of ordinary QI numerical bonuses and enchant potential, with integer rounding and fractional sockets still unresolved; later magical implements will share this philosophy. Starting Heroes are granted one eligible Junk weapon and one Junk armor ensemble even if their combined appraised value exceeds the level1 30-Gem reference target. No extra armor breakage rule, bonus penalty to shield block, or new stat threshold is implied by this update.

## November 11, 2026 — Ordinary arrow consumption and contextual recovery (AUTHOR APPROVED)

For any legal ordinary bow attack, **one prepared arrow is consumed from the archer's carried ready ammunition count at the moment of firing**. Both previously approved Piercing and Slashing ordinary arrowheads use the same ordinary projectile category, and choosing a different prepared arrow does not create an extra reload turn or a free attack. An arrow that has left the quiver is **not automatically destroyed**: after combat, intact physically accessible arrows can be retrieved if circumstances permit, subject to GM adjudication of actual collision, damage and terrain. **No mandatory recovery percentage, independent arrow durability track, or guaranteed automatic recovery.**

Ordinary standard quiver capacity is **20** arrows (Piercing and Slashing any mixture); a Gem Shop's **40-arrow resupply bundle costs 10 Gems** and has no quiver. These are logistics and price rules, not adjustments to shot Power, quadratic draw, range boundaries, Armor Rating bypass, Aim, or action economy. See `27_Ranged_Weapon_Catalogue_DRAFT.md` and `09_Equipment_and_Economy.md`. Bolt/cartridge economics and special ammunition remain separate and unpriced.

## November 11, 2026 — Traditional arrow tiers removed; legendary bow working Powers and handling approved (LATEST AUTHOR CORRECTION)

**LATEST, HIGHEST-PRECEDENCE AUTHOR DECISION:** The author **rejects multiple sizes or numerical draw-force tiers of traditional arrows** as unnecessary rule/inventory complexity, while expressly approving the remaining immediately proposed legendary-bow handling and Power recommendations. **Supersedes** the previous working 100/200/300/400 arrow safe-launch rating categories, the ammunition-derived maximum draw `R`, the rated-arrow fallback for intentional underdraw, and any supposed need to buy, carry, sort, or match an arrow to the bow's cap by a separate ammunition tier. All previous four-tier arrow-ratings/784-case ammo availability results are **historical tests, not operative ammunition mechanics**; do not run them as though they were approved after this correction.

### Approved bow working calibration

| Distinct bow design (working name) | Engineered draw cap | Minimum controlled partial-draw effective STR | Minimum Handling DEX | Full-draw **Piercing** shot Power |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Advanced high-draw bow | **100** | **35** | **30** | **14,500** (previously approved) |
| Titan bow | **200** | **80** | **35** | **45,000** (now approved working rating) |
| Colossal composite bow | **300** | **125** | **40** | **90,000** (now approved working rating) |
| Legendary greatbow | **400** | **180** | **45** | **150,000** (now approved working rating) |

**Eligibility:** For an ordinary traditional bowshot, meet the listed Handling DEX and the bow's minimum safe controlled draw STR. Choose any actual draw `D_actual` satisfying `minimum controlled draw ≤ D_actual ≤ min(effective shooter STR, this bow's engineered draw cap)`. Apply the **approved quadratic partial-draw equation** `P_shot = P_full_for_this_specific_bow_and_projectile_form × (D_actual / this bow's engineered cap)^2`. More STR than this *particular* bow cap does not add damage, but a stronger engineered bow can use more STR. A controlled partial draw uses less Power; it is **not** an automatic extra attack or a constant-Power minimum. Aim/hit accuracy continues to use DEX and standard range/cover rules, not DEX as an added Power factor.

**Traditional ammunition deliberately simple:** A normal bowshot consumes **one ordinary arrow**, with **no tracking of arrow size classes or rated launch-force tiers**. For this game's ordinary traditional archery, assume routine arrows are usable with the above traditional bows without imposing a new per-bow ammunition-size prerequisite, separate ammo STR cap, or 'must buy greatbow arrows' inventory tax. The **only presently approved ordinary arrowhead damage-form distinction** remains **Slashing broadhead (F1.10, 0% covered AR bypass)** versus **dedicated Piercing point (F0.90, 50% covered AR bypass)** at the same legal draw. The listed full-draw Power values in this table describe the Piercing load; the previously approved squared relative-factor relationship `P_piercing / P_slashing = 81/121` remains, with no added quality/material damage multiplier. Broadhead/piercing form is a choice of **head/attack behavior, not an arrow size/tension tier**. Do not infer another ammunition count, cost, strength class, standardized projectile mass or special size requirement from a bow's descriptor.

**Physical commonsense and limits:** A bow still needs actual space to draw/fire and cannot shoot without its ammunition or through a physical barrier. An unusual nontraditional projectile mechanism may require an expressly developed item-specific exception in a later pass, but **do not reintroduce traditional-arrow size ratings as a covert fit, craftsmanship, materials, stock or Quality gate**. Exact weapon material construction, Quality requirements, final item naming, mass, range bands, rarity, Gem cost and any extraordinary ammo remain separate/unresolved.

**Tempo:** One arrow per ordinary bowshot and the existing bow nock/draw pace. There is **no additional mandatory reload turn, free Aim, automatic additional attack, firearm-style recoil Strain, mandatory bracing/scope or magical glint** solely for being a high-cap bow. Existing approved 10-meter firearm burst, advanced crossbow and arbalest bolt/cocking, precision firearm/cartridge and advanced-optic rules are unaffected. Bolt and firearm ammunition identities are not collapsed into arrows.

**Future balance docket (previously approved deferment):** Keep the observed high-END/armor marginal contribution on a later **legendary-heroes game-testing checklist**; do not change armor, END² or the ordinary Piercing bypass now.

**History:** Earlier November 11 passages in this and cross-referenced files that called the four safe-arrow launch-force tiers author-approved are **specifically superseded by this latest correction**. Historical 784-case experiments remain an archive of a discarded ammo-tier hypothesis; earlier **bow-only** quadratic, handling, draw-cap and Power calibration remains usable.

## November 11, 2026 — Approved conventional Slashing and Piercing projectile forms; ranged reload timing

**Ranged ammunition form comparison:** For a comparable physically compatible ordinary bow arrow (or engineered compatible crossbow bolt), the **Slashing broadhead primary form F1.10** has **0% base armor bypass** and the **dedicated Piercing point primary form F0.90** has **50% covered-AR bypass**. Use the **already approved melee primary form reference factors**, each once with the same underlying actual draw or mechanical firing source. At equal applicable input, `P_piercing = P_slashing × (0.90/1.10)^2 = P_slashing × 81/121`. Thus Piercing raw Power is 33.06% lower than comparable Slashing Power, while proper Piercing halves covered AR *before* its term is squared in `END² + effective AR²`. Do **not** treat relative F as a flat final-Power multiplier, use a greatsword secondary thrust adjustment, assume Slashing has penetration, or stack a second 50% bonus on any already Piercing firearm shot.

**Existing approved shot Power is intact:** The advanced full-draw bow **14,500**, advanced crossbow **17,000** and arbalest **26,000** test anchors were evaluated with genuine Piercing projectile loads and 50% bypass. For matched comparison, Slashing counterparts are approximately 21,660 / 25,395 / 38,840, **derived mathematical comparators**, not separate newly bought weapon ratings. Advanced precision rifle **15,000** and light precision firearm **11,800** remain previously tested Piercing-load benchmarks; no unrelated Slashing cartridge model or special firearm penetration mode is introduced. The latest author correction **abolishes** the former four-rated-arrow size/force tiers; no traditional arrow rating enters this comparison. Slashing and Piercing head forms remain separate attack forms at the same legal bow draw.

**Now-approved advanced equipment operation:** Advanced crossbow Support STR28, Handling DEX30; hand-cock STR90 or suitable lever STR40; one loaded bolt, one reload turn. Advanced arbalest remains Support STR70, DEX40, hand-cock STR170 or winch STR70, one heavy loaded bolt, two reload turns and one deployment setup turn; **normal reloading does not reset setup**, but genuinely losing/abandoning deployment does. Advanced precision rifle Support STR40, Handling DEX50, Recoil STR70, standard magazine5; light precision firearm Support STR22, DEX40, Recoil STR38, standard magazine6; each one cartridge per ordinary single-shot action, full nonshooting magazine change one turn. A magazine is not automatic-fire eligibility and does not grant free consecutive attacks. All existing recoil ratio, strain reset, cover, Aim, optic glint, PP and range-eligibility rules remain unchanged.

**User's specialty-ammo design boundary:** Do not create numerical explosion, freeze, elemental or magical ammunition packages in this pass; defer them until underlying spell combat mechanics can be compared fairly. No extra AR multiplier, blast radius, status chance or magic damage bonus is conferred on the two physical projectile forms by this approval.
